## `AFG-7320-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** Blind-Taste-Test Metrologist
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Survey Sample Response Screener & Statistical Cross-Tabulation
